2026-10-29T16:23:52.3560361Z   QWEN_COMMIT_MESSAGE: [require-build] [cluster:firmus] [nodes:2] [prefill-pp4] Serve the round-2 PP4 bundle with a fresh image

One image build and one PP4 serving run (c225, no [profile]) for the round-2
bundle on top of the 9392.85 configuration (40883389891, image
40849459077). The image must be rebuilt: the DeepGEMM host series gained
sm90-prefill-direct-fc2.patch and sm90-prefill-gather-a.patch (so
check-firmus-image.sh rejects 40849459077) and kernels/fa3-verify compiles
the two-CTA rows. Under [require-build] provision rewrites the PP4 pins
(container_*) to this run's image and check-firmus-pp4-cpu.py accepts it
because its sidecar names this commit (e7153e3).

Changes in the bundle and expected effect (power-cap factor in brackets)
1. GPU order (f0b51e2): the last, lightest PP4 stage of each Prefill worker
   runs on that worker's slowest GPU (configs/firmus-gpu-sm-clock.json,
   order-firmus-pp4-gpus.py, CUDA_VISIBLE_DEVICES in worker.sh); prefill-0
   is unchanged and is the in-run control. prefill-1 +0.5-0.7%, prefill-2
   +0.5-1.1%, Prefill capacity +0.35-0.6% [1: the work moves to a faster
   GPU]. Model c225 9404 -> 9458.
2. MegaMoE four-stage adoption on total saving (699ae8f, not yet served):
   bit-exact, 2.1% of MegaMoE in the replays of both archived runs, about
   -1.4 ms per stage [1].
3. MegaMoE direct FC2 stores and EP1 A gather (24a98f1, 860d4ef; host +
   header): -1.5..-3.0 ms per 24-layer stage on top of four-stage [~1,
   work removal; shared-memory and HBM bytes].
4. FP32 Prefill Q/K norm + RoPE + FP8 writer (7eb80b0,
   SGLANG_PREFILL_QKNORM=fp32): -47% executed instructions, -1.2..-1.6 ms
   per stage [~1, instruction removal]; no coarser format, Q/K equal to the
   FP64 result rounded once on 99.99994% of elements in CPU emulation.
5. Staggered QKV/O DeepGEMM 1D2D schedule (7eb80b0,
   SGLANG_PREFILL_DENSE_GEMM=schedule): 0..-1.9 ms per stage [0.36-0.6,
   idle removal, already applied], bit-exact by construction.
6. Two-CTA-per-SM Decode FA3 verify rows (08d87e4): -1.1..-2.1 ms per
   Decode step (-3..-5.5%) [~1, latency-bound kernel]; split2-c2 writes the
   pinned split2-s3 bytes.
7. Pinned PP4 selections (7cb9864): Prefill FA3 n192-direct-o-varlen,
   Prefill MoE input fused, Decode norm/quant wide without SwapAB, the same
   winners as both runs that selected them; no kernel change against
   40883389891, about 45 s less pre-model-load time.
8. Decode cap 48 per DP rank (196fca7): decode_max_running_requests 192,
   graph sizes 46/48; neutral at c225, removes the ~50.7 req/s cap-44
   ceiling that a faster Prefill would hit. The two-CTA FA3 rows must win
   at 44, 46 and 48.

Prefill period from 2+3+4+5: -3.7 / -6.0 / -7.9 ms per stage (low / mid /
high, x0.970 / x0.951 / x0.935). Serving model at c225, cap 48, Decode
x1.00 / x0.96 / x0.95: 9533 / 9932 / 10063 (slope 0.72 ms/request;
9682 / 9959 / 10139 with 0.30). c230-235 would add 0.2-0.5% only at mid/high
and lose up to 1.5% at low, so concurrency stays 225.

In-job gates (each falls back to the control or the pin)
- Provision: PP4 CPU gate on the new image (overlay chain of 28 SGLang
  overlays applied to the extracted tree, PP relay/loop/EAGLE contracts,
  prepare-firmus-pp4-settings.py), check-firmus-image.sh (host series
  recorded in the image equals patches/deep-gemm-host/series).
- Prefill GPUs (three workers in parallel): select-firmus-prefill-megamoe.py
  with control, four-stage, four-stage-split, four-stage-direct,
  four-stage-gather and four-stage-direct-gather; FP32 reference for the
  control (a failure stops the job), exactness on every GPU and routing,
  summed margin; a missing or late result keeps three stages.
- Decode GPUs, one after the other: (1) select-firmus-prefill-attention.py
  (qknorm: V and NaN bit-equal, finite Q/K = FP64 rounded once on >= 99.99%
  with at most one E4M3 step; dense: QKV and O bit-equal; faster on all four
  GPUs by max(3 us, 2%) per layer; 240 s timeout); (2)
  select-firmus-decode-kernels.sh fa3 --pinned-env (numeric gate, byte
  equality for equal splits, >= 1 us/layer in both blocks on every GPU at
  44, 46 and 48; 175 s timeout); (3) check-firmus-pp4-copy-engine.py (exact
  transfers on four GPUs, 60 s timeout).
- Workers: order-firmus-pp4-gpus.py keeps the order if a UUID is missing;
  check-firmus-server-info.py checks the Decode cap 192 and graph sizes; the
  copy-engine transport validates 16 launches before trusting copies.

Timeline estimate (t = job start; from 40883389891 and 40825417205)
- 40883389891: MoE input torchrun t+25, MegaMoE t+31, dense t+94, Prefill
  FA3 screen t+126, copy engine t+180, workers about t+206 (first log lines
  t+231), Prefill ready t+377, Decode ready t+398, router start t+467,
  READY t+469, AIPerf t+508..t+748, job end t+760 (12:40).
- This run: Prefill-GPU chain MegaMoE about 91 s + 15-20 s for the three new
  variants -> t+137..142. Decode-GPU chain attention 65-95 s, FA3 two-CTA
  25-45 s, copy engine about 25 s from t+20 -> t+135..185. Workers about
  t+160 (140-185); Decode ready about launch + 179 s + 7 s for graph sizes
  46/48; router start about 68 s after Decode ready (both archived runs) ->
  READY about t+430 (410-455); AIPerf +39 s, 240 s; job end about t+720 s
  (12:00; 11:40-12:25).
- A [profile] pass costs about 242 s (40825417205: main AIPerf end 14:44:57
  to job end 14:49:11, less the 12 s an ordinary job spends after AIPerf)
  plus about 26 s of slower worker start under nsys (load to READY 271 s
  against 245 s) -> about t+990 s, 30 s (5-55 s) below the 17-minute limit,
  short of the 90 s margin. Not requested.
- If all three Decode-GPU selections ran into their timeouts (240 + 175 +
  60 s) the workers would start near t+510 and the job would exceed the
  limit; the previous chain had the same exposure (150 + 420 + 60 s).

- results/score.json: eligible, score against 9392.85; TTFT failures
  (127, all in the first 10 s) and ITL failures (2) in the AIPerf export.
- results/prefill-megamoe/selection.json: winner and total saving; every
  variant's exactness on all twelve GPUs; the env line in prefill-kernels.env.

  source/prefill-kernels.env (after FA3_VERIFY=n192-direct-o-varlen,

  block, bytes_equal_to_control for split2-c2; source/decode-kernels.env
  ends with the winner (otherwise the five pinned lines with NORM_QUANT=wide).

  Prefill log "PP4 proxy transport: copy (this stage ok after 16 validated
  launches)" for PP0-PP3.
- Prefill logs: gpu= order (prefill-1 and prefill-2 with their slowest GPU

  120.8 / 120.2 / 123.9 ms; expect 112-117 ms and ratios prefill-1/prefill-0

  ms.
- Timing: READY after about 430 s, Slurm elapsed about 12:00.
